    $ cat frontend/vite.config.ts && sed -n '1,180p' backend/evolution_engine/verification_bus.py
    import { defineConfig } from 'vite'
    import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react'
    
    export default defineConfig({ plugins: [react()], server: { proxy: { '/api': 'http://127.0.0.1:8000', '/ws': { target: 'ws://127.0.0.1:8000', ws: true } } } })
    """Verification subscriber consuming the MongoDB job bus.
    
    The subscriber gets references and public invariants from the bus; only the
    trusted execution layer maps a registered commit to a controller handle.
    Individual black-box verifiers execute with separate OS restrictions.  This
    module is a coordinator, not itself an information-theoretic airgap.
    """
    from __future__ import annotations
    
    import hashlib
    from pathlib import Path
    import queue
    import subprocess
    import tempfile
    import threading
    import time
    
    from backend.physics_sim.worlds import evaluate_isolated
    from backend.verifier_agent.mutation import audit
    
    
    class VerificationSubscriber:
        """Watch insertions and evaluate only explicitly registered commit handles."""
    
        def __init__(self, store, invariants):
            self.store = store
            self.invariants = dict(invariants)
            self.stop_event = threading.Event()
            self.ready = threading.Event()
            self.condition = threading.Condition()
            self.handles = {}
            self.results = {}
            self.error = None
            self.tmpdir = None
            self.thread = None
    
        def __enter__(self):
            self.tmpdir = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="lazarus-verify-handles-")
            self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self._watch, name="verification-bus", daemon=True)
            self.thread.start()
            if not self.ready.wait(15):
                self.close()
                raise RuntimeError("Verification bus did not become ready")
            if self.error:
                error = self.error
                self.close()
                raise RuntimeError("Verification bus could not start") from error
            return self
    
        def __exit__(self, *args):
            self.close()
    
        def close(self):
            self.stop_event.set()
            if self.thread:
                self.thread.join(timeout=65)
                if self.thread.is_alive():
                    # Do not remove an executable handle while its worker is using it.
                    raise RuntimeError("Verification bus did not stop")
            if self.tmpdir:
                self.tmpdir.cleanup()
                self.tmpdir = None
    
        def register(self, repo, commit):
            """Resolve a known commit to an immutable private local worker artifact."""
            if not self.tmpdir or self.stop_event.is_set():
                raise RuntimeError("Verification bus is not running")
            # Never accept a source path from the job itself. Read the object from
            # the skill repository at registration, before exposing its identifier.
            result = subprocess.run(
                ["git", "-C", str(repo), "show", f"{commit}:.skills/controllers/active_skill.py"],
                check=True, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
            )
            content = result.stdout
            if not content:
                raise ValueError("Empty committed controller")
            digest = hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest()
            artifact = Path(self.tmpdir.name) / f"{digest}.py"
            artifact.write_bytes(content)
            entrypoint = "controller-step:" + commit
            with self.condition:
                self.handles[entrypoint] = (commit, artifact, digest)
            return entrypoint
    
        def wait(self, job, timeout=120):
            """Wait only for the exact inserted job's verdict; do not poll MongoDB."""
            deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
            with self.condition:
                while job["job_id"] not in self.results:
                    if self.error:
                        raise RuntimeError("Verification subscriber failed") from self.error
                    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
                    if remaining <= 0:
                        raise TimeoutError("Verification job was not delivered")
                    self.condition.wait(remaining)
                result = self.results.pop(job["job_id"])
            if isinstance(result, Exception):
                raise RuntimeError("Verification failed; candidate not certified") from result
            return result
    
        def _dispatch(self, job):
            entrypoint = job.get("blackbox_entrypoint_id")
            with self.condition:
                handle = self.handles.get(entrypoint)
            if handle is None:
                # Other deployments may share the same collection. They are not
                # allowed to make this process execute arbitrary filesystem paths.
                return
            commit, artifact, digest = handle
            job_id = job.get("job_id")
            try:
                if not isinstance(job_id, str) or not job_id:
                    raise ValueError("Missing job ID")
                if job.get("git_commit_hash") != commit or job.get("plc_topology_id") != "plc-demo-v1":
                    raise ValueError("Verification job does not match registered handle")
                if job.get("status") != "PENDING" or job.get("safety_invariants_spec") != self.invariants:
                    raise ValueError("Verification job has invalid invariants or status")
                if hashlib.sha256(artifact.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != digest:
                    raise RuntimeError("Committed controller artifact changed")
                worlds = evaluate_isolated(artifact)
                mutation = audit(artifact)
                if mutation.get("candidate_sha256") != digest or hashlib.sha256(artifact.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != digest:
                    raise RuntimeError("Verified controller artifact changed")
                result = {"world_result": worlds, "audit": mutation, "candidate_sha256": digest}
            except Exception as exc:
                result = exc
            with self.condition:
                self.results[job_id] = result
                self.condition.notify_all()
    
        def _watch(self):
            events = None
            try:
                if self.store.db is None:
                    events = queue.Queue()
                    with self.store.lock:
                        self.store._subscribers.append(events)
                    self.ready.set()
                    while not self.stop_event.is_set():
                        try:
                            job = events.get(timeout=0.5)
                        except queue.Empty:
                            continue
                        with self.store.lock:
                            self.store.counters["change_events"] += 1
                        self._dispatch(job)
                else:
                    collection = self.store.db.airgapped_verification_bus
                    with collection.watch([{"$match": {"operationType": "insert"}}],
                                          full_document="updateLookup", max_await_time_ms=1000) as stream:
                        # collection.watch establishes the stream before returning,
                        # closing the otherwise easy-to-miss insert/subscribe race.
                        self.ready.set()
                        while not self.stop_event.is_set():
                            change = stream.try_next()
                            if change:
                                with self.store.lock:
                                    self.store.counters["change_events"] += 1
                                self._dispatch(change["fullDocument"])
            except Exception as exc:
                with self.condition:
                    self.error = exc
                    self.condition.notify_all()
                self.ready.set()
            finally:
                if events is not None:
                    with self.store.lock:
                        if events in self.store._subscribers:
                            self.store._subscribers.remove(events)

    ## LAZARUS-TWIN `frontend/src` — read-only review
    
    Read [App.tsx](/root/LAZARUS-TWIN/frontend/src/App.tsx), [WorkbenchScene.tsx](/root/LAZARUS-TWIN/frontend/src/WorkbenchScene.tsx), [types.ts](/root/LAZARUS-TWIN/frontend/src/types.ts), [main.tsx](/root/LAZARUS-TWIN/frontend/src/main.tsx), [style.css](/root/LAZARUS-TWIN/frontend/src/style.css), [index.html](/root/LAZARUS-TWIN/frontend/index.html), [vite.config.ts](/root/LAZARUS-TWIN/frontend/vite.config.ts).
    
    ### P0 — likely wrong at runtime
    
    1. **Terminal pins stack on top of each other.** `pinX()` in [WorkbenchScene.tsx](/root/LAZARUS-TWIN/frontend/src/WorkbenchScene.tsx) only spreads slots 1 and 3; any other slot returns `slotX` exactly. Multiple terminals in slot 2 (or 0/4 from real inspection data) render at identical coordinates — unclickable, z-fighting LEDs.
    2. **Thermal-spike button sends stale `enabled` flag.** [App.tsx](/root/LAZARUS-TWIN/frontend/src/App.tsx) calls `sabotage(!!snapshot.sabotage_active, true, false)`. On first load `sabotage_active` is `undefined`, so a thermal spike posts `enabled:false + thermal_spike:true` — likely cancels the fault it means to inject. Reset is the only call with deliberate `(false,false,false)`.
    3. **`twinValue` drops numeric strings.** [types.ts](/root/LAZARUS-TWIN/frontend/src/types.ts) only accepts `number|boolean`. If the API returns `"89.5"` or `"1"`, every readout (`num(...)`, `signal()`, e-stop, temperature) falls back to `—` / `0` instead of parsing.
    
    ### P1 — usability / correctness
    
    4. **No deselect on canvas; tiny click targets.** `onSelect` only ever sets a terminal; empty-space clicks do nothing (`onPointerMissed` missing). Screw heads are `r=0.1` meshes with no keyboard focus, and `document.body.style.cursor` is mutated directly with no cleanup on unmount.
    5. **Arm snaps to 0 on telemetry dropout.** `RobotArm` uses `twinValue(...) ?? 0` every frame, so a missing frame drives both arms toward zero instead of holding last angle. Angle wrap (e.g. ±180°) is also unhandled, causing a full sweep via `damp`.
    6. **Inspector always shows right-evolved twin.** `PinInspector` receives `snapshot.right_evolved` for every pin, so legacy-controller faults are invisible in the inspector; stale `previous` terminal is also retained when it disappears from `topology.terminals`.
    7. **3D geometry leak on re-inspect.** `Cable` memoizes `TubeGeometry` but never disposes it. Each `refreshInspection` with new terminals leaks GPU buffers.
    8. **Per-frame allocations in `useFrame`.** `new THREE.Quaternion()` + `new THREE.Vector3()` per arm per frame; plus a 150 ms `setInterval(()=>setSnapshot({...frame.current}))` that re-renders all DOM overlays ~7×/s even with no new WS data.
    
    ### P2 — polish / edge cases
    
    - WebSocket URL rebuild overwrites `pathname` with `/ws`, discarding any path prefix in `VITE_API_URL`.
    - `signal()` divides by fixed 90/100 scales with no clamp; `Math.min(1,…)` hides it in the LED but `trackError` can show large signed values while the up/down arrow is static per side.
    - `eq.pass_rate*100` renders `NaN%` when `vectors=0`; `safeText(undefined)` renders `'""'`; unknown `EvidenceState` makes the priority sort comparator `NaN`.
    - No WebGL-failure / Rapier-load error boundary (`Suspense fallback={null}`), no `#root` placeholder or `<noscript>` — failure is a black/blank viewport.
    - `pin-inspector` overlays the top-right canvas and blocks orbit gestures there; on ≤700 px the workcell labels, VS badge, and inspector overlap.
    - `request()` forces `Content-Type: application/json` on GETs (extra preflight); Mongo stats poll every 2.5 s with no visibility-gating or abort.

    ## Findings — `backend/rea_archaeologist` + `backend/prober` (read-only)
    
    Top issue first. All verified by reading the four source files plus `targets/legacy_controller.c` (used only to check FFI/threshold claims).
    
    ### P0 — Evidence gap: probed artifact is not the hashed artifact
    - [build.py](/root/LAZARUS-TWIN/backend/rea_archaeologist/build.py:18) builds binary and `.so` with different flags, but returns `sha256` of the **binary only**.
    - [ffi.py](/root/LAZARUS-TWIN/backend/prober/ffi.py:10) and therefore all of `probe.py` / `equivalence()` execute the **library**, which has no recorded digest.
    - [inspect.py](/root/LAZARUS-TWIN/backend/rea_archaeologist/inspect.py:18) checks the REA digest against the binary hash only. So the disassembly evidence and the dynamic-test evidence are about two different files with no link between them.
    
    ### P0 — `load_skill()` is unauthenticated arbitrary code execution
    - [probe.py](/root/LAZARUS-TWIN/backend/prober/probe.py:10) does `spec_from_file_location` + `exec_module` on a caller-supplied `path` (default `.skills/.../active_skill.py`), with no hash/allow-list/sandbox. A malicious or stale skill file gets full process privileges. Same risk in `equivalence()`'s fixed template path if that file is writable.
    
    ### P1 — `CDLL` load has no integrity check + TOCTOU
    - [ffi.py](/root/LAZARUS-TWIN/backend/prober/ffi.py:10): `CTDLL(str(path))` trusts whatever is in writable `build/` at load time. `build()`'s atomic `os.replace` protects readers already mapped, but a swap between `build()` returning the path and `CDLL` opening it executes attacker code. No digest, signature, owner/permission, or `RTLD_NOW` hardening.
    
    ### P1 — Reliability: probe failures crash instead of returning evidence
    - [probe.py](/root/LAZARUS-TWIN/backend/prober/probe.py:28) raises bare `AssertionError` when the `[0, 1]` transition is absent (float rounding, controller change, hysteresis). One flaky sample aborts all boundaries; should return `UNKNOWN`/failed-finding.
    - Same file: only 2 samples per boundary at hardcoded `71.999999/72.0`, `87.999999/88.0`, `45.0/45.000001`. Correct per the C source (`>= 72`, `>= 88`, `fabs(error) > 45`), but `confidence: 1.0` on n=2 with no repeats or binary-search refinement overstates the threshold location. Negative-error side (`error < -45`) is never probed.
    - `equivalence()`: one exception (NaN, missing `candidate.step`, divergence) aborts all 1000 vectors; mismatches truncated to 10 (lossy); `rel_tol=1e-12` will flag algebraically-equivalent rewrites as failures. Non-finite safe-state path (`estop=1, cooling=1` in C) is never sampled since `uniform()` can't emit NaN/Inf.
    
    ### P1 — Accuracy overstatement: "gains" are single raw samples
    - [probe.py](/root/LAZARUS-TWIN/backend/prober/probe.py:31): `proportional_sample` and `velocity_sample` are one `pwm` reading each (numerically 3.0 / −0.8 per the C law `3.0*error − 0.8*velocity`, clamped ±100). Labels imply identified gains; no isolation from clamping, no second point to prove linearity, no test near saturation.
    
    ### P2 — Fragile objdump parsing presented at `confidence 1.0`
    - [inspect.py](/root/LAZARUS-TWIN/backend/rea_archaeologist/inspect.py:24): single regex over REA `stdout`; any format/architecture change silently yields zero rows (then hits the `not floating or not jumps` error) or mis-parsed rows. Heuristics `sd/ss/xmm` and `startswith("j")` are x86-centric and substring-based.
    - `SEEN` items keep only `{offset, mnemonic, operands}` — raw line discarded, so the claim can't be re-audited. `instruction_count` is returned but never cross-checked against evidence length.
    - Untrusted REA JSON is used without schema validation (`raw_result.stdout`, `evidence_id`, `provider` all bare-keyed → `KeyError` crash); `provider` is echoed but never authenticated, so a compromised local `rea.mjs` can mint "evidence".
    - Evidence cache write `(ROOT / "build/rea_evidence.json").write_text` is non-atomic, unlocked, silently overwritten.
    
    ### P2 — Build lock is process-local; subprocess has no timeout
    - [build.py](/root/LAZARUS-TWIN/backend/rea_archaeologist/build.py:11): `_LOCK = threading.RLock()` serializes threads in one process only. Two processes can still run the check-then-build race (line 19); outcome is benign (atomic replace, last-writer-wins) but wastes compiles and the returned digest may describe a superseded file.
    - Four `subprocess.run(..., check=True)` calls have no `timeout` (a hung `gcc`/`strip` hangs `build()`, `inspect()`, and every `LegacyController()` construction). No compiler-version pinning, so bytes (and the digest) can shift across hosts. Missing-source raises unhandled `FileNotFoundError` from `source.stat()`.
    - `shutil.copy2` to `.vault/` on every call, even when nothing rebuilt; no integrity check on the vault copy.
    
    What they get right (so you don't "fix" it away): digest cross-check before parsing, `CONTROL_LAW` correctly marked `INFERRED 0.35`, thermal/cooling/runaway unknowns explicitly `UNKNOWN 0.0`, topology terminals `state UNKNOWN / offset None` with a "proposed, not recovered" note, and `equivalence()` scope honestly labeled "not a proof".

    """Actual sponsor API clients, with explicitly labeled local fallback decisions.
    
    These are advisory services; none of their outputs bypasses the verifier.
    """
    import math
    import os
    
    import httpx
    from dotenv import dotenv_values
    
    from backend.rea_archaeologist.build import ROOT
    
    
    class Router:
        def __init__(self, offline=False):
            self.values = {**dotenv_values(ROOT / ".env"), **os.environ}
            self.offline = offline or self.values.get("LAZARUS_OFFLINE") == "1"
            self.status = {
                "meta": "configured" if self._meta_key() else "not_configured",
                "subquadratic": "configured" if self._subq_key() else "not_configured",
            }
            if self.offline:
                self.status = {name: "offline" for name in self.status}
    
        def _meta_key(self):
            return self.values.get("META_API_KEY") or self.values.get("MODEL_API_KEY")
    
        def _subq_key(self):
            return self.values.get("SUBQUADRATIC_API_KEY") or self.values.get("SUBQ_API_KEY")
    
        @staticmethod
        def _failure(exc):
            # Do not include request/response bodies or exception strings: they may contain secrets.
            if isinstance(exc, httpx.HTTPStatusError):
                return "unavailable: HTTP " + str(exc.response.status_code)
            return "unavailable: " + type(exc).__name__
    
        def meta(self, prompt, fallback):
            """Use Muse Spark through Chat Completions, or return the provided local result."""
            key = self._meta_key()
            if not self.offline and key:
                try:
                    response = httpx.post(
                        "https://api.meta.ai/v1/chat/completions",
                        headers={"Authorization": "Bearer " + key},
                        json={"model": self.values.get("META_MODEL", "muse-spark-1.3"),
                              "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}], "max_tokens": 512},
                        timeout=8,
                    )
                    response.raise_for_status()
                    answer = response.json()["choices"][0]["message"]["content"]
                    if not isinstance(answer, str) or not answer.strip():
                        raise ValueError("Empty model response")
                    self.status["meta"] = "connected"
                    return {"provider": "meta", "text": answer}
                except (httpx.HTTPError, KeyError, ValueError, TypeError, IndexError) as exc:
                    self.status["meta"] = self._failure(exc)
            return {"provider": "local", "text": fallback}
    
        def subq_decision(self, state, instruction, fallback=False):
            """Ask SubQ Snap's Decisions API; the fallback is not a model probability."""
            key = self._subq_key()
            if not self.offline and key:
                try:
                    response = httpx.post(
                        "https://api.subq.ai/v1/systemone",
                        headers={"Authorization": "Bearer " + key},
                        json={"model": "subq-snap-1.1", "state": state,
                              "questions": {"decision": {"type": "noul", "instructions": instruction,
                                "criteria": {"true": "The condition is supported by the evidence.",
                                             "false": "The condition is not supported by the evidence."}}}},
                        timeout=8,
                    )
                    response.raise_for_status()
                    answer = response.json()["answers"]["decision"]["noul"]
                    if isinstance(answer, bool) or not isinstance(answer, (float, int)) or not math.isfinite(answer) or not 0 <= answer <= 1:
                        raise ValueError("Invalid SubQ decision probability")
                    self.status["subquadratic"] = "connected"
                    return {"provider": "subquadratic", "probability": float(answer), "decision": answer >= 0.5}
                except (httpx.HTTPError, KeyError, ValueError, TypeError, IndexError) as exc:
                    self.status["subquadratic"] = self._failure(exc)
            return {"provider": "local", "probability": None, "decision": bool(fallback)}
    
        def connections(self):
            return dict(self.status)

        def assess_evidence(self, evidence):
            """Offer model analysis alongside deterministic evidence counts; never certify code."""
            counts = evidence.get("counts", {})
            context = json.dumps(evidence, sort_keys=True, default=str)
            prompt = ("Assess this controller evidence. Distinguish observations from hypotheses, "

                subq = executor.submit(self.subq_decision, context,
                    "Does the evidence contain an explicitly UNKNOWN branch?",
                    bool(counts.get("UNKNOWN", 0)))

    from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
    from backend.db.store import Store
    import backend.main as main
    
    
    def test_api_and_websocket_with_local_storage(monkeypatch,tmp_path):
        monkeypatch.setattr(main,'Store',lambda:Store(force_offline=True,local_path=tmp_path/'db.json'))
        with TestClient(main.app) as client:

            assert client.post('/api/sabotage',json={'enabled':True,'thermal_spike':True}).status_code==200
            with client.websocket_connect('/ws') as socket:
                first=socket.receive_json()
                # The heat is latched on the next 100Hz physics scan.
                for _ in range(5):
                    if first['right_evolved']['estop']:break
                    first=socket.receive_json()
                assert first['sabotage_active']
                assert first['right_evolved']['estop']==1
                assert first['right_evolved']['pwm']==0
            reset=client.post('/api/sabotage',json={'enabled':False,'thermal_spike':False})
            assert reset.status_code==200
            assert reset.json()['snapshot']['right_evolved']['estop']==0

        """Black-box boundary probes and sampled differential tests, not a formal proof."""
        import importlib.util
        import math
        import random
        import subprocess
        from .ffi import LegacyController
        from backend.rea_archaeologist.build import ROOT, build
        
        
        def load_skill(path=None):
            spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("skill", str(path or ROOT / ".skills/controllers/active_skill.py"))
            module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
            spec.loader.exec_module(module)
            return module
        
        
        def probe(controller=None):
            legacy = controller or LegacyController()
            specs = [
                ("COOLING_BOUNDARY", "cooling", [(0,0,0,71.999999), (0,0,0,72.0)]),
                ("THERMAL_BOUNDARY", "estop", [(0,0,0,87.999999), (0,0,0,88.0)]),
                ("RUNAWAY_BOUNDARY", "estop", [(45.0,0,0,20), (45.000001,0,0,20), (-45.0,0,0,20), (-45.000001,0,0,20)]),
            ]
            findings = []
            for node_id, channel, inputs in specs:
                samples = [{"input": list(v), "output": legacy.step(*v)} for v in inputs]
                observed=[s["output"][channel] for s in samples]
                expected=[0,1] if len(inputs)==2 else [0,1,0,1]
                matches=observed==expected
                findings.append({"node_id":node_id, "state":"SEEN" if matches else "UNKNOWN", "confidence":1.0 if matches else 0.0,
                                 "payload":{"samples":samples,"scope":"observed samples only; transition lies between samples when present"}})
            gains = {"proportional_sample":legacy.step(1,0,0,20)["pwm"], "velocity_sample":legacy.step(0,0,1,20)["pwm"],
                     "scope":"single unsaturated input-output samples, not a proof of linearity"}
            return {"findings":findings, "gains":gains}
        
        
        def equivalence(count=1000, seed=2026):
            if count < 1:raise ValueError("count must be positive")
            artifacts=build()
            legacy = LegacyController(artifacts["library"])
            candidate = load_skill(ROOT / "backend/evolution_engine/templates/v1.py")
            rng = random.Random(seed)
            edges=[(0,0,0,72),(0,0,0,88),(45,0,0,20),(45.000001,0,0,20),(-45,0,0,20),(-45.000001,0,0,20),
                   (float('nan'),0,0,20),(0,0,float('inf'),20),(0,0,0,float('-inf'))]
            vectors=edges[:count]
            while len(vectors)<count:
                if len(vectors)%2:
                    vectors.append((rng.uniform(-40,40),0,rng.uniform(-100,100),rng.uniform(0,87.9)))
                else:
                    vectors.append((rng.uniform(-90,90),rng.uniform(-90,90),rng.uniform(-250,250),rng.uniform(-20,130)))
            mismatches=[]
            for i, vector in enumerate(vectors):
                ffi, recovered = legacy.step(*vector), candidate.step(*vector)
                completed=subprocess.run([artifacts['binary'],*[repr(v) for v in vector]],capture_output=True,text=True,check=True,timeout=3)
                p,e,c=completed.stdout.split()
                executable={'pwm':float(p),'estop':int(e),'cooling':int(c)}
                def same(a,b):
                    return a['estop']==b['estop'] and a['cooling']==b['cooling'] and math.isclose(a['pwm'],b['pwm'],rel_tol=1e-12,abs_tol=1e-9)
                if not same(ffi,recovered) or not same(executable,ffi):
                    mismatches.append({'index':i,'input':[str(v) for v in vector],'executable':executable,'ffi':ffi,'recovered':recovered})
            return {'vectors':count,'seed':seed,'passed':count-len(mismatches),'mismatch_count':len(mismatches),'mismatches':mismatches[:10],
                    'pass_rate':(count-len(mismatches))/count,'binary_sha256':artifacts['sha256'],'library_sha256':artifacts['library_sha256'],
                    'scope':'Sampled comparison of the stripped executable, shared library and baseline Python; not a proof for all inputs.'}
        
        if __name__ == "__main__":
            import json
            print(json.dumps({"probes":probe(), "equivalence":equivalence()}, indent=2))
        EOF

        from pathlib import Path
        import fcntl
        import hashlib
        import json
        import os
        import shutil
        import subprocess
        import tempfile
        import threading
        
        ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
        BUILD = ROOT / "build"
        _LOCK=threading.RLock()
        
        def digest(path):
            return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
        
        def build():
            """Build atomically; never truncate a shared library mapped by a running twin."""
            with _LOCK:
                BUILD.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
                with (BUILD / '.build.lock').open('a+') as lock:
                    fcntl.flock(lock,fcntl.LOCK_EX)
                    source = ROOT / "targets/legacy_controller.c"
                    binary, library = BUILD / "legacy_controller", BUILD / "legacy_controller.so"
                    manifest=BUILD / 'artifact_manifest.json'
                    source_sha=digest(source)
                    try: cached=json.loads(manifest.read_text())
                    except (OSError,ValueError):cached={}
                    valid=(binary.exists() and library.exists() and cached.get('source_sha256')==source_sha
                           and cached.get('binary_sha256')==digest(binary) and cached.get('library_sha256')==digest(library))
                    if not valid:
                        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="build-",dir=BUILD) as tmp:
                            tmp_binary=Path(tmp)/"controller"; tmp_library=Path(tmp)/"controller.so"
                            subprocess.run(["gcc", "-O2", "-fno-ident", "-o", str(tmp_binary), str(source), "-lm"], check=True, timeout=60)
                            subprocess.run(["strip", "--strip-all", str(tmp_binary)], check=True, timeout=30)
                            subprocess.run(["gcc", "-O2", "-fPIC", "-shared", "-o", str(tmp_library), str(source), "-lm"], check=True, timeout=60)
                            subprocess.run(["strip", "--strip-unneeded", str(tmp_library)], check=True, timeout=30)
                            cached={'source_sha256':source_sha,'binary_sha256':digest(tmp_binary),'library_sha256':digest(tmp_library)}
                            os.replace(tmp_binary,binary);os.replace(tmp_library,library)
                            tmp_manifest=Path(tmp)/'manifest.json';tmp_manifest.write_text(json.dumps(cached))
                            os.replace(tmp_manifest,manifest)
                    vault = ROOT / ".vault"
                    vault.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
                    sealed=vault / "legacy_controller.c"
                    if not sealed.exists() or digest(sealed)!=source_sha:shutil.copy2(source,sealed)
                    return {"binary":str(binary),"library":str(library),"sha256":cached['binary_sha256'],
                            "library_sha256":cached['library_sha256'],"source_sha256":source_sha}
        
        if __name__ == "__main__":
            print(json.dumps(build(), indent=2))
        EOF

    #!/usr/bin/env bash
    # Install development dependencies only when missing; never alter .env.
    set -euo pipefail
    cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
    for tool in python3 gcc strip objdump nm git node npm; do
      if ! command -v "$tool" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
        printf 'Missing required tool: %s\n' "$tool" >&2
        exit 1
      fi
    done
    if [[ ! -x .venv/bin/python ]]; then
      python3 -m venv .venv
    fi
    if ! .venv/bin/python -c 'import fastapi,uvicorn,pymongo,dns,dotenv,httpx,pytest,uagents,websockets' >/dev/null 2>&1; then

    """Offline evaluation worlds. Parameters are held out from the live workcell.
    
    These are deterministic benchmark fixtures, not physically sealed secret worlds.
    """
    from .engine import Arm, World
    
    WORLDS=(
        World("Payload mass",efficiency=.40,gravity=26,inertia=2.4),
        World("Low friction",efficiency=.45,gravity=24,inertia=1.7,friction=.3),
        World("High inertia + sensor lag",efficiency=.42,gravity=25,inertia=4.5,friction=.6,sensor_lag_s=.45),
        World("Thermal stress",efficiency=.46,gravity=22,inertia=2.2,friction=1,initial_temp=80),
        World("Combined perturbations",efficiency=.38,gravity=26,inertia=3,friction=1.1,sensor_lag_s=.18),
    )
    
    def evaluate(controller, steps=3000):
        scores=[]
        for number,world in enumerate(WORLDS,1):
            arm=Arm(controller,world)
            peak=0.0
            errors=[]
            for i in range(steps):
                s=arm.step()
                peak=max(peak,s["angle"]-arm.target)
                if i>=steps-200:errors.append(abs(s["tracking_error_deg"]))
            steady=sum(errors)/len(errors)
            passed=steady<2.2 and peak<4.5 and not arm.estop
            reasons=[]
            if steady>=2.2:reasons.append("steady-state error >= 2.2°")
            if peak>=4.5:reasons.append("overshoot >= 4.5°")
            if arm.estop:reasons.append("emergency stop latched")
            scores.append({"world":number,"name":world.name,"passed":passed,"steady_state_error_deg":round(steady,3),
                           "peak_overshoot_deg":round(peak,3),"estop":arm.estop,"reasons":reasons})
        return scores
    
    def evaluate_isolated(path):
        """Start the held-out evaluator with an execution handle, not candidate source."""
        import json
        import os
        import subprocess
        import sys
        from backend.rea_archaeologist.build import ROOT
        env={"PATH":os.environ.get("PATH",""),"LANG":"C.UTF-8"}
        worker=subprocess.Popen([sys.executable,str(ROOT/"backend/evolution_engine/controller_worker.py"),str(path)],
                                stdin=subprocess.PIPE,stdout=subprocess.PIPE,stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,env=env)
        try:
            write_fd,read_fd=worker.stdin.fileno(),worker.stdout.fileno()
            result=subprocess.run([sys.executable,"-m","backend.physics_sim.isolated_world_runner",str(write_fd),str(read_fd)],
                                  cwd=ROOT,pass_fds=(write_fd,read_fd),capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=60,env=env)
            if result.returncode:raise RuntimeError("isolated world evaluation failed: "+result.stderr[-600:])
            return json.loads(result.stdout)
        finally:
            worker.terminate()
            try:worker.wait(timeout=3)
            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:worker.kill();worker.wait()
            worker.stdin.close();worker.stdout.close()
    """Real Git skill evolution, with fail-closed benchmark and mutation gates.
    
    Git and the database are separate systems; promotion is a compensating saga,
    not a cross-system atomic transaction. Git operations occur in an isolated
    skill repository, never by resetting or reverting the user's main worktree.
    """
    from datetime import datetime, timezone
    import fcntl
    import hashlib
    import os
    from pathlib import Path
    import shutil
    import subprocess
    import threading
    import uuid
    
    from backend.physics_sim.engine import Arm, World, load_skill
    from backend.rea_archaeologist.build import ROOT
    from backend.evolution_engine.verification_bus import VerificationSubscriber
    
    TEMPLATES=Path(__file__).parent / "templates"
    INVARIANTS={"thermal_cutoff_c":88.0,"cooling_c":72.0,"runaway_deg":45.0,"pwm_min":-100.0,"pwm_max":100.0,
                "steady_error_deg":2.2,"overshoot_deg":4.5}
    
    
    def _training(controller):
        arm=Arm(controller,World())
        peak=0.0; tail=[]
        for i in range(3000):
            sample=arm.step()
            peak=max(peak,sample["angle"]-arm.target)
            if i>=2800:tail.append(abs(sample["tracking_error_deg"]))
        error=sum(tail)/len(tail)
        return {"steady_state_error_deg":round(error,3),"peak_overshoot_deg":round(peak,3),"estop":arm.estop,
                "passed":error<4.0 and peak<4.5 and not arm.estop}
    
    class EvolutionEngine:
        def __init__(self,sim=None,store=None,base_dir=None,active_path=None):
            if store is None:
                from backend.db.store import Store
                store=Store()
            self.sim=sim; self.store=store
            self.base_dir=Path(base_dir or ROOT / ".data/skill_repositories")
            self.active_path=Path(active_path or ROOT / ".skills/controllers/active_skill.py")
            self.lock=threading.Lock()
    
        @staticmethod
        def git(repo,*args):
            command=["git","-C",str(repo),"-c","user.name=LAZARUS Skill Architect", "-c","user.email=skill-architect@lazarus.local",*args]
            return subprocess.run(command,check=True,text=True,capture_output=True).stdout.strip()
    
        def _commit(self,repo,label):
            self.git(repo,"add",".skills/controllers/active_skill.py")
            self.git(repo,"commit","--quiet","-m",label)
            return self.git(repo,"rev-parse","HEAD")
    
        def _job(self,commit):
            if self.store is None:return None
            return self.store.create_job(commit,"controller-step:"+commit,"plc-demo-v1",INVARIANTS)
    
        def _verdict(self,job,record,active,status):
            if self.store is not None:
                # The store owns the MongoDB multi-document transaction.
                self.store.commit_verdict(job["job_id"],record,active,status)
    
        def _recall(self):
            if self.store is None:return {"status":"not_configured","hits":[]}
            query=[.58,.42,25.,4.5,.45,1.2,30.,1.]
            pattern={"pattern_id":"gravity-lag-pid","pattern_type":"PHYSICAL_FAILURE_COMPENSATOR",
                     "symptom_summary":"Torque loss with gravity sag and oscillation under sensor lag",
                     "embedding":query,
                     "recommended_skill_template":"Clamped integral, cosine gravity feedforward, filtered velocity",
                     "verification_status":"candidate; evaluate before use"}
            try:
                self.store.save_pattern(pattern)
                hits=self.store.vector_search(query)
                return {"status":"atlas_vector_search" if self.store.mode=="atlas" else "local_cosine", "hits":hits}
            except Exception as exc:
                # Do not claim Atlas recall or silently replace the database when its index is unavailable.
                return {"status":"unavailable", "reason":type(exc).__name__, "hits":[]}
    
        def run(self):
            with self.lock:
                self.base_dir.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
                with open(self.base_dir / ".evolution.lock","a+") as lock_file:
                    fcntl.flock(lock_file,fcntl.LOCK_EX)
                    try:
                        with VerificationSubscriber(self.store, INVARIANTS) as subscriber:
                            return self._run(subscriber)
                    finally:fcntl.flock(lock_file,fcntl.LOCK_UN)
    
        def _run(self, subscriber):
            repo=self.base_dir/uuid.uuid4().hex
            repo.mkdir(parents=True)
            self.git(repo,"init","--quiet")
            skill=repo/".skills/controllers/active_skill.py"
            skill.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
            shutil.copyfile(TEMPLATES/"v1.py",skill)
            v1=self._commit(repo,"v1 recovered baseline")
            baseline=load_skill(skill)
            v1_train=_training(baseline.step)
            ledger=[{"version":"v1","status":"BASELINE","commit":v1,"training":v1_train}]
    
            shutil.copyfile(TEMPLATES/"v2.py",skill)
            v2=self._commit(repo,"v2 aggressive training candidate")
            subscriber.register(repo, v2)
            job2=self._job(v2)
            overfit=load_skill(skill)
            training2=_training(overfit.step)
            verification2=subscriber.wait(job2)
            world_result2=verification2["world_result"]
            worlds2=world_result2["worlds"]
            audit2=verification2["audit"]
            rejected=not training2["passed"] or not all(w["passed"] for w in worlds2) or not audit2["baseline"]["passed"] or audit2["killed"]<audit2["total"]*.9
            if not rejected:
                raise RuntimeError("v2 unexpectedly passed; refusing to fabricate a rollback")
            self.git(repo,"revert","--no-edit",v2)
            revert=self.git(repo,"rev-parse","HEAD")
            v2_record={"version":"v2","status":"REVERTED","commit":v2,"revert_commit":revert,"training":training2,
                       "worlds":worlds2,"world_verifier_isolation":world_result2["isolation"],"audit":audit2,"reason":"held-out benchmark or verifier rejected candidate",
                       "timestamp":datetime.now(timezone.utc)}
            self._verdict(job2,v2_record,revert,"REJECTED_TRIGGER_GIT_REVERT")
            # PyMongo mutates inserted dicts to add ObjectIds; keep JSON responses clean.
            v2_record.pop("_id",None); v2_record["timestamp"]=v2_record["timestamp"].isoformat()
            ledger.append(v2_record)
            recall=self._recall()
    
            shutil.copyfile(TEMPLATES/"v3.py",skill)
            v3=self._commit(repo,"v3 adaptive anti-windup controller")
            subscriber.register(repo, v3)
            job3=self._job(v3)
            adaptive=load_skill(skill)
            verification3=subscriber.wait(job3)
            world_result3=verification3["world_result"]
            worlds3=world_result3["worlds"]
            audit3=verification3["audit"]
            if not all(w["passed"] for w in worlds3) or not audit3["baseline"]["passed"] or audit3["killed"] < audit3["total"]*.9:
                self.git(repo,"revert","--no-edit",v3)
                fail_commit=self.git(repo,"rev-parse","HEAD")
                record={"version":"v3","status":"REVERTED","commit":v3,"worlds":worlds3,"audit":audit3}
                self._verdict(job3,record,fail_commit,"REJECTED_TRIGGER_GIT_REVERT")
                raise RuntimeError("v3 failed safety or held-out benchmark; reverted")
            v3_record={"version":"v3","status":"ADOPTED","commit":v3,"worlds":worlds3,"world_verifier_isolation":world_result3["isolation"],"audit":audit3,
                       "timestamp":datetime.now(timezone.utc)}
            # Publish the approved code atomically; restore the previous file if the database transaction fails.
            self.active_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
            prior=self.active_path.read_bytes() if self.active_path.exists() else None
            tmp=self.active_path.with_name(self.active_path.name+".new")
            candidate_bytes=skill.read_bytes()
            if hashlib.sha256(candidate_bytes).hexdigest()!=verification3["candidate_sha256"]:
                raise RuntimeError("Candidate differs from verified commit; refusing hot-swap")
            tmp.write_bytes(candidate_bytes); os.replace(tmp,self.active_path)
            try:self._verdict(job3,v3_record,v3,"CERTIFIED_FOR_HOT_SWAP")
            except Exception:
                if prior is None:self.active_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
                else:
                    tmp.write_bytes(prior);os.replace(tmp,self.active_path)
                raise
            if self.sim is not None:self.sim.adopt(self.active_path)
            v3_record.pop("_id",None);v3_record["timestamp"]=v3_record["timestamp"].isoformat()
            ledger.append(v3_record)
            return {"iterations":ledger,"active_skill_version":"v3","active_commit":v3,"revert_commit":revert,
                    "repository":str(repo),"memory_recall":recall,
                    "note":"Git skill repository and MongoDB are coordinated as a saga, not one atomic transaction."}

        // Test the supplied runtime artifact, not a freshly compiled replacement.
        const fs = require('fs');
        const path = require('path');
        const net = require('net');
        const crypto = require('crypto');
        const solc = require('solc');
        const { spawn } = require('child_process');
        const { ethers } = require('ethers');
        const root = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
        const artifactPath=path.join(root,'contracts/legacy_governor.hex');
        const runtime=fs.readFileSync(artifactPath,'utf8').trim();
        if (!/^0x(?:[a-fA-F0-9]{2})+$/.test(runtime)) throw Error('Invalid supplied EVM runtime artifact');
        const artifactSha256=crypto.createHash('sha256').update(Buffer.from(runtime.slice(2),'hex')).digest('hex');
        const source='contracts/RecoveredGovernor.sol';
        const input={language:'Solidity',sources:{[source]:{content:fs.readFileSync(path.join(root,source),'utf8')}},settings:{optimizer:{enabled:true,runs:200},outputSelection:{'*':{'*':['abi','evm.bytecode.object']}}}};
        const compiled=JSON.parse(solc.compile(JSON.stringify(input)));
        const errors=(compiled.errors||[]).filter(e=>e.severity==='error');
        if (errors.length) throw Error(errors.map(e=>e.formattedMessage).join('\n'));
        const recovered=compiled.contracts[source].RecoveredGovernor;
        const delay=ms=>new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,ms));
        (async()=>{
          const port=await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const s=net.createServer();s.on('error',reject);s.listen(0,'127.0.0.1',()=>{const p=s.address().port;s.close(()=>resolve(p));});});
          const proc=spawn(path.join(root,'node_modules/node/bin/node'), [path.join(root,'node_modules/@foundry-rs/anvil/bin.mjs'),'--host','127.0.0.1','--port',String(port),'--silent'],{stdio:'ignore'});
          try {
            await delay(300);
            if (proc.exitCode!==null) throw Error('Local Anvil exited before startup');
            const provider=new ethers.JsonRpcProvider(`http://127.0.0.1:${port}`,undefined,{staticNetwork:true});
            let ready=false;
            for (let i=0;i<60;i++) {if(proc.exitCode!==null) throw Error('Local Anvil exited during startup');try{await provider.send('eth_chainId',[]);ready=true;break;}catch{await delay(100)}}
            if(!ready) throw Error('Local Anvil did not start');
            const signer=await provider.getSigner(0);
            const legacyAddress='0x000000000000000000000000000000000000beef';
            await provider.send('anvil_setCode',[legacyAddress,runtime]);
            const a=new ethers.Contract(legacyAddress,recovered.abi,signer);
            const b=await new ethers.ContractFactory(recovered.abi,recovered.evm.bytecode.object,signer).deploy();await b.waitForDeployment();
            let state=2026;const rand=()=>{state=(state*1664525+1013904223)>>>0;return state;};
            const samples=[[0,0,0,71999],[0,0,0,72000],[0,0,0,87999],[0,0,0,88000],[45000,0,0,20000],[45001,0,0,20000]];
            for(let i=0;i<256;i++) samples.push([(rand()%180001)-90000,(rand()%180001)-90000,(rand()%500001)-250000,rand()%130001]);
            for(const args of samples){
              const [x,y]=await Promise.all([a.step(...args),b.step(...args)]);
              if(x[0]!==y[0]||x[1]!==y[1]||x[2]!==y[2]) throw Error(`EVM mismatch for ${args}`);
            }
            console.log(JSON.stringify({network:'local Anvil',artifact_sha256:artifactSha256,tested:samples.length,passed:samples.length,legacy:legacyAddress,recovered:await b.getAddress()}));
            provider.destroy();
          } finally {proc.kill('SIGTERM');}
        })().catch(err=>{console.error(err.message);process.exitCode=1;});
        EOF

    # LAZARUS-TWIN
    
    LAZARUS-TWIN is a **demo engineering workbench** for inspecting a stripped industrial-controller artifact, comparing a recovered controller with it, and exercising a safety-gated control-skill evolution loop. It renders a PLC cabinet and two robot arms in a WebSocket-driven 3D scene. The supplied target binaries and contracts are demonstration artifacts compiled from the source in this repository, not recovered vendor software.
    
    ## Run the demo
    
    On Linux, install Python 3 with `venv`, Node/npm, Git, GCC, `strip`, `nm`, and GNU `objdump`, then run:
    
    ```bash
    ./scripts/start_demo.sh
    ```
    
    The setup script creates `.venv` and installs missing Python and Node dependencies. It installs `rea-agents` as an **external npm package** and uses a local Node 22 executable for REA. The first setup needs package-registry access. Open <http://localhost:5173>; the API listens on port 8010. Press Ctrl-C to stop both services. Ports must be free, and the Vite proxy expects port 8010.
    
    Select **Probe Unknown Branches** to run the REA/objdump inspection, controller boundary sweeps, and 1,000-vector comparison. Use the sabotage controls to load the arm, trigger the evolution sequence, or test the thermal stop. Reset clears the simulated emergency latch.
    
    ## Configure integrations
    
    Place optional credentials in an untracked `.env` in the repository root. Never commit or share the file:
    
    ```dotenv
    # Choose either a complete URI, or the connection string and credentials below.
    MONGODB_URI=
    MONGODB_CONNECT=
    MONGODB_USER=
    MONGODB_PASS=
    
    META_API_KEY=
    META_MODEL=muse-spark-1.3
    SUBQUADRATIC_API_KEY=
    # Optional: disable live Meta and SubQ calls (does not disable MongoDB).
    LAZARUS_OFFLINE=1
    ```
    
    With a reachable MongoDB deployment the application uses the `lazarus_twin_db` database, including a time-series collection, graph traversal, vector search, change streams and transactional audit updates. When no MongoDB URI is configured or the server is unreachable at startup, it uses a local JSON store under `.data/`. A reachable deployment with insufficient permissions or unsupported features reports an error rather than pretending that an Atlas operation succeeded. Atlas Vector Search requires its `skill_memory_vector` search index to finish provisioning before recall works; index status is not a successful recall. Configuring credentials does not itself prove provider connectivity.
    
    Active probing sends a summary of the demo evidence to Meta and Subquadratic when their keys are configured. Set `LAZARUS_OFFLINE=1` to use the explicitly labeled deterministic model fallbacks; this setting does not disable MongoDB. SubQ Snap is called on the Decisions API. Local Fetch.ai uAgents carry workbench jobs among the archaeologist, architect, verifier, and gateway. No agent registration with an external Fetch.ai service is required for local operation.
    
    The MongoDB change-stream subscriber dispatches registered verification jobs to the isolated execution layer. The websocket displays events; audit verdict, job status, and active controller pointer are updated together in a MongoDB transaction. Git and MongoDB cannot share a transaction, so evolution uses a compensating workflow across the two systems.
    
    ## Verify
    
    ```bash
    ./scripts/verify_all.sh
    ```
    
    This compiles and checks the stripped executable, invokes external REA with GNU objdump, executes boundary probes and 1,000 seeded stripped-executable-versus-shared-library-versus-Python vectors, runs six AST mutants through the restricted verifier, exercises the Git commit/revert/adopt sequence and five simulation fixtures, tests REST and WebSocket paths with an isolated local data store, runs EVM comparisons on a real local Anvil instance, and builds the frontend. It fails if required tools, dependencies, are unavailable. These checks do not require Atlas connectivity or mutate the configured Atlas database.
    
    To inspect service state while the demo runs:
    
    - `GET /api/health` — active skill, Git commit, provider configuration and telemetry errors.
    - `POST /api/archaeology/inspect` — pass `{"run_active_probes": true}` to run the sweeps and differential test.
    - `POST /api/sabotage` — accepts `enabled`, `thermal_spike`, and `auto_evolve` booleans.
    - `POST /api/evolve` and `POST /api/verify` — run the evolution loop and mutation audit.
    - `GET /api/mongodb/stats` — storage mode and database counters.
    - `WS /ws` — twin telemetry and verification-job events.
    
    ## Boundaries and interpretation
    
    - This is a **simulation and research demo**, not a PLC safety system or an authorization to operate machinery. Physical parameters and the PLC layout are demo assumptions. Wiring and assembly-to-pin relationships without evidence remain unknown; sampled behavior does not prove actual wiring.
    - The 1,000-vector check is **sampled differential testing**, not a formal proof of equivalence. EVM testing uses a separately compiled demonstration target on a local Anvil node, with integer-scaled inputs. It does not establish equivalence for all EVM inputs.
    - The five deterministic benchmark worlds are held out from the live workcell but their fixtures are readable in this repository; they are not secret or physically sealed. Evolution records real commits and a real revert inside an isolated Git skill repository under `.data/skill_repositories/`, then copies the approved skill to `.skills/controllers/`.
    - The verifier process is denied filesystem access with Linux Landlock and blocks network and process-inspection syscalls with seccomp. Its black-box controller worker and mutation auditor are separate processes, but this is not an information-theoretic airgap or a substitute for independent adversarial infrastructure. The receipt uses a local HMAC key, not a third-party signature. Linux Landlock ABI 3+ and `libseccomp` are required; the verifier fails closed when isolation cannot be established.
    - The backend performs fixed-step rotational and thermal simulation. The frontend uses Rapier kinematic rigid bodies driven by the backend's telemetry. The 100 Hz simulation and 60 Hz broadcast are best-effort scheduling targets; frame rate depends on host and browser performance.
    - `.env`, generated targets, local audit data, skill repositories, virtual environments, and dependencies are ignored by Git. The target source remains visible under `targets/`; the `.vault` copy is a demo artifact, not a protected secret vault.
    """Deterministic, fixed-step rotational rigid-body and thermal simulation.
    
    Units: angle degrees, angular velocity degrees/s, PWM percentage. The inertia,
    friction, motor efficiency and gravity coefficient are tunable model parameters,
    not measurements from real equipment.
    """
    from collections import deque
    from dataclasses import dataclass
    import importlib.util
    import math
    from pathlib import Path
    import threading
    import uuid
    from backend.prober.ffi import LegacyController
    from backend.rea_archaeologist.build import ROOT
    
    DT = 0.01
    
    @dataclass(frozen=True)
    class World:
        name: str = "live"
        efficiency: float = 0.42
        gravity: float = 25.0
        inertia: float = 2.0
        friction: float = 1.2
        sensor_lag_s: float = 0.0
        initial_temp: float = 30.0
    
    
    def load_skill(path):
        spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("lazarus_skill_" + uuid.uuid4().hex, str(path))
        if spec is None or spec.loader is None: raise RuntimeError("cannot load skill")
        module=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
        spec.loader.exec_module(module)
        if not callable(getattr(module,"step",None)): raise ValueError("skill has no step function")
        return module
    
    
    class Arm:
        def __init__(self, controller, world=World(), target=30.0):
            self.controller=controller
            self.world=world
            self.target=target
            self.angle=0.0
            self.velocity=0.0
            self.temperature=world.initial_temp
            self.pwm=0.0
            self.estop=0
            self.cooling=0
            self.state={}
            self.delay=deque([0.0]*max(1,round(world.sensor_lag_s / DT)))
            self.sabotage=True
            self.ticks=0
    
        def step(self):
            measured=self.delay.popleft()
            self.delay.append(self.angle)
            out=self.controller(self.target, measured, self.velocity, self.temperature, self.state)
            pwm=float(out["pwm"])
            if not math.isfinite(pwm): raise ValueError("controller returned non-finite PWM")
            self.estop=int(bool(self.estop or out["estop"]))
            self.cooling=int(bool(out["cooling"] or self.temperature >= 88.0))
            self.pwm=0.0 if self.estop else max(-100.0,min(100.0,pwm))
            efficiency=self.world.efficiency if self.sabotage else 1.0
            gravity=self.world.gravity if self.sabotage else 8.0
            acceleration=(efficiency*self.pwm - gravity*math.cos(math.radians(self.angle)) - self.world.friction*self.velocity)/self.world.inertia
            self.velocity += acceleration*DT
            self.angle += self.velocity*DT
            # Clamp to physical stop so a tripped arm cannot fall forever.
            if self.angle < -90.0: self.angle=-90.0; self.velocity=max(0.0,self.velocity)
            if self.angle > 90.0: self.angle=90.0; self.velocity=min(0.0,self.velocity)
            self.temperature += (abs(self.pwm)*0.0005 - (0.025 if self.cooling else 0.0))*DT
            self.ticks+=1
            return self.snapshot()
    
        def snapshot(self):
            return {"target_angle":self.target, "angle":self.angle, "velocity":self.velocity,
                    "temperature":self.temperature, "pwm":self.pwm, "estop":self.estop,
                    "cooling":self.cooling, "tracking_error_deg":self.target-self.angle,
                    "sabotage_active":self.sabotage}
    
    
    class DualTwin:
        def __init__(self, skill_path=None):
            self.lock=threading.RLock()
            self.legacy=LegacyController()
            self.skill_path=Path(skill_path or ROOT / ".skills/controllers/active_skill.py")
            self.skill=load_skill(self.skill_path)
            self._reset()
    
        def _reset(self):
            self.tick=0
            self.left=Arm(self.legacy.step)
            self.right=Arm(self.skill.step)
            self.left.sabotage=self.right.sabotage=False
    
        def reset(self):
            with self.lock:self._reset()
            return self.snapshot()
    
        def set_fault(self, enabled, thermal_spike=False):
            with self.lock:
                self.left.sabotage=self.right.sabotage=bool(enabled)
                if thermal_spike:self.left.temperature=self.right.temperature=89.5
            return self.snapshot()
    
        def adopt(self,path):
            candidate=load_skill(Path(path))
            with self.lock:
                self.skill=candidate
                self.skill_path=Path(path)
                self.right.controller=candidate.step
                self.right.state={}
            return candidate.VERSION
    
        def step(self,steps=1):
            if not 0 <= steps <= 10000:raise ValueError("steps out of range")
            with self.lock:
                for _ in range(steps):
                    self.left.step(); self.right.step(); self.tick+=1

    $ cat frontend/src/WorkbenchScene.tsx
    import { Canvas, useFrame } from '@react-three/fiber'
    import { Grid, Html, Line, OrbitControls, RoundedBox } from '@react-three/drei'
    import { Physics, RigidBody, type RapierRigidBody } from '@react-three/rapier'
    import { Suspense, useMemo, useRef, type MutableRefObject } from 'react'
    import * as THREE from 'three'
    import { type Frame, type Terminal, twinValue } from './types'
    
    const palette = { SEEN: '#54e6a2', INFERRED: '#ffba5b', UNKNOWN: '#f4505a' }
    const modules = [
      { code: 'PS 24V', tag: 'POWER', hue: '#5e717b' }, { code: 'AI 16', tag: 'ANALOG IN', hue: '#2e5961' },
      { code: 'PWM 01', tag: 'SERVO', hue: '#48695a' }, { code: 'SR 02', tag: 'SAFETY', hue: '#807452' },
      { code: 'EVM 01', tag: 'BRIDGE', hue: '#4a5973' },
    ]
    const slotX = (slot: number) => (slot - 2) * 1.65
    const pinX = (pin: Terminal, i: number) => slotX(pin.slot) + (pin.slot === 1 ? (i - 1.5) * 0.27 : pin.slot === 3 ? (i - .5) * .4 : 0)
    const signal = (pin: Terminal, frame: Frame): number => {
      const t = frame.right_evolved
      switch (pin.id) {
        case '%IW0': return Math.abs(twinValue(t, 'target', 'target_angle') ?? 0) / 90
        case '%IW1': return Math.abs(twinValue(t, 'angle', 'angle_deg', 'current_angle') ?? 0) / 90
        case '%IW2': return Math.abs(twinValue(t, 'velocity', 'angular_velocity') ?? 0) / 100
        case '%IW3': return Math.abs(twinValue(t, 'temperature', 'temp', 'core_temp_c') ?? 0) / 100
        case '%QW0': return Math.abs(twinValue(t, 'pwm', 'motor_pwm') ?? 0) / 100
        case '%QX0.0': return twinValue(t, 'estop') ?? 0
        case '%QX0.1': return twinValue(t, 'cooling') ?? 0
        default: return 0
      }
    }
    
    function ScrewPin({ pin, x, frame, selected, onSelect }: { pin: Terminal; x: number; frame: MutableRefObject<Frame>; selected: boolean; onSelect: (terminal: Terminal) => void }) {
      const led = useRef<THREE.MeshStandardMaterial>(null)
      useFrame(({ clock }) => { if (led.current) led.current.emissiveIntensity = .35 + Math.min(1, signal(pin, frame.current)) * 1.6 + .18 * (1 + Math.sin(clock.elapsedTime * 3)) })
      const color = palette[pin.state] ?? palette.UNKNOWN
      return <group position={[x, 5.48, .8]} onClick={e => { e.stopPropagation(); onSelect(pin) }} onPointerOver={e => { e.stopPropagation(); document.body.style.cursor = 'pointer' }} onPointerOut={() => { document.body.style.cursor = 'auto' }}>
        <mesh><cylinderGeometry args={[.1,.1,.08,20]} /><meshStandardMaterial color={selected ? '#f0fffd' : '#777d72'} metalness={.9} roughness={.28} /></mesh>
        <mesh position={[0,.07,.04]}><sphereGeometry args={[.065,12,12]} /><meshStandardMaterial ref={led} color={color} emissive={color} emissiveIntensity={1} /></mesh>
        <mesh position={[0,.115,.075]}><boxGeometry args={[.09,.018,.018]} /><meshBasicMaterial color="#dce5d9" /></mesh>
      </group>
    }
    
    function Cable({ pin, start, end, index }: { pin: Terminal; start: [number, number, number]; end: [number, number, number]; index: number }) {
      const color = palette[pin.state] ?? palette.UNKNOWN
      const curve = useMemo(() => new THREE.CubicBezierCurve3(new THREE.Vector3(...start), new THREE.Vector3(start[0], 4.55-index*.045, 1.15), new THREE.Vector3(end[0], 4.05-index*.11, .5), new THREE.Vector3(...end)), [start[0], start[1], start[2], end[0], end[1], end[2], index])
      const points = useMemo(() => curve.getPoints(40), [curve])
      const tube = useMemo(() => new THREE.TubeGeometry(curve, 48, .026, 5, false), [curve])
      if (pin.state === 'UNKNOWN') return <Line points={points} color={color} lineWidth={1.8} dashed dashSize={.14} gapSize={.1} transparent opacity={.9} />
      return <mesh geometry={tube}><meshStandardMaterial color={color} emissive={color} emissiveIntensity={pin.state === 'SEEN' ? .9 : .35} roughness={.3} /></mesh>
    }
    
    function Cabinet({ terminals, frame, selected, onSelect }: { terminals: Terminal[]; frame: MutableRefObject<Frame>; selected: string | null; onSelect: (terminal: Terminal) => void }) {
      const perSlot = new Map<number, number>()
      const pins = terminals.map(pin => { const i = perSlot.get(pin.slot) ?? 0; perSlot.set(pin.slot, i + 1); return { pin, x: pinX(pin, i) } })
      return <group>
        <RoundedBox args={[9.15,2.45,.42]} radius={.08} position={[0,6.06,-.12]}><meshStandardMaterial color="#1a292e" metalness={.72} roughness={.35} /></RoundedBox>
        <mesh position={[0,7.26,.1]}><boxGeometry args={[8.8,.07,.16]} /><meshStandardMaterial color="#839397" metalness={.85} roughness={.28} /></mesh>
        <mesh position={[0,5.12,.2]}><boxGeometry args={[8.8,.12,.18]} /><meshStandardMaterial color="#89999a" metalness={.9} roughness={.3} /></mesh>
        {[0,1,2,3,4].map(slot => <group position={[slotX(slot),6.13,.25]} key={slot}>
          <RoundedBox args={[1.44,1.74,.68]} radius={.045}><meshStandardMaterial color="#c3c4af" metalness={.3} roughness={.45} /></RoundedBox>
          <mesh position={[0,.58,.36]}><boxGeometry args={[1.3,.42,.06]} /><meshStandardMaterial color={modules[slot].hue} metalness={.3} roughness={.5} /></mesh>
          <mesh position={[0,-.39,.36]}><boxGeometry args={[1.3,.34,.07]} /><meshStandardMaterial color="#aeb7a9" metalness={.3} /></mesh>
          {[0,1,2,3,4].map(n=><mesh key={n} position={[-.52+n*.26,-.12,.363]}><boxGeometry args={[.055,.33,.025]} /><meshStandardMaterial color="#76897e" /></mesh>)}
          <mesh position={[-.5,.6,.405]}><sphereGeometry args={[.045,10,10]} /><meshStandardMaterial color="#9dff9e" emissive="#71ffab" emissiveIntensity={1.2} /></mesh>
          <Html position={[0,.57,.44]} center style={{ pointerEvents:'none' }}><div className="module-3d-label"><b>{modules[slot].code}</b><span>{modules[slot].tag}</span></div></Html>
          <Html position={[0,1.1,.1]} center style={{ pointerEvents:'none' }}><span className="module-slot">0{slot}</span></Html>
        </group>)}
        {pins.map(({pin,x})=><ScrewPin key={pin.id} pin={pin} x={x} frame={frame} selected={selected === pin.id} onSelect={onSelect} />)}
        {pins.map(({pin,x},i)=><Cable key={'wire-'+pin.id} pin={pin} index={i} start={[x,5.42,.72]} end={[i % 2 ? 3.4 : -3.4, 2.3 + i*.06, .15]} />)}
      </group>
    }
    
    function RobotArm({ side, frame }: { side: 'left'|'right'; frame: MutableRefObject<Frame> }) {
      const arm = useRef<RapierRigidBody>(null)
      const current = useRef(0)
      const warning = useRef<THREE.MeshStandardMaterial>(null)
      const x = side === 'left' ? -3.35 : 3.35
      const accent = side === 'left' ? '#7caec0' : '#60d9a1'
      useFrame((_, delta) => {
        const twin = side === 'left' ? frame.current.left_legacy : frame.current.right_evolved
        const angle = twinValue(twin, 'angle', 'angle_deg', 'current_angle') ?? 0
        current.current = THREE.MathUtils.damp(current.current, -angle * Math.PI/180, 8, delta)
        const q = new THREE.Quaternion().setFromAxisAngle(new THREE.Vector3(0,0,1), current.current)
        arm.current?.setNextKinematicRotation({ x:q.x, y:q.y, z:q.z, w:q.w })
        if (warning.current) {
          const temp = twinValue(twin, 'temperature', 'temp', 'core_temp_c') ?? 20
          warning.current.emissive.set(temp >= 88 ? '#ff4a39' : temp >= 72 ? '#f9a448' : side === 'left' ? '#3285aa' : '#3fb884')
          warning.current.emissiveIntensity = temp >= 88 ? 2 : .65
        }
      })
      return <group position={[x,0,0]}>
        <RigidBody type="fixed" colliders="cuboid"><mesh position={[0,.05,0]} receiveShadow><boxGeometry args={[4.7,.22,3]} /><meshStandardMaterial color="#1c2c30" metalness={.65} roughness={.42} /></mesh></RigidBody>
        <mesh position={[0,.18,0]}><boxGeometry args={[4.55,.04,2.85]} /><meshStandardMaterial color="#3b5557" metalness={.8} roughness={.35} /></mesh>
        {[-1,1].map(n => <mesh key={n} position={[n*1.9,.2,0]}><boxGeometry args={[.08,.04,2.5]} /><meshStandardMaterial color="#82a4a3" metalness={.8} /></mesh>)}
        <mesh position={[0,.52,0]}><cylinderGeometry args={[.66,.78,.65,28]} /><meshStandardMaterial color="#566768" metalness={.75} roughness={.3} /></mesh>
        <mesh position={[0,.87,0]}><cylinderGeometry args={[.46,.59,.17,28]} /><meshStandardMaterial color={accent} emissive={accent} emissiveIntensity={.2} metalness={.45} /></mesh>
        <RigidBody ref={arm} type="kinematicPosition" position={[0,1.04,0]} colliders="cuboid">
          <mesh position={[0,1.05,0]} castShadow><boxGeometry args={[.42,2.15,.44]} /><meshStandardMaterial color="#bcc4ae" metalness={.58} roughness={.32} /></mesh>
          <mesh position={[0,1.05,.24]}><boxGeometry args={[.21,1.7,.06]} /><meshStandardMaterial color={accent} metalness={.6} /></mesh>
          <mesh position={[0,2.15,0]}><cylinderGeometry args={[.34,.34,.5,24]} /><meshStandardMaterial color="#52696a" metalness={.75} /></mesh>
          <mesh position={[0,2.54,0]} castShadow><boxGeometry args={[.62,.55,.62]} /><meshStandardMaterial color="#d29e47" metalness={.58} roughness={.32} /></mesh>
          <mesh position={[0,2.54,.32]}><boxGeometry args={[.36,.12,.02]} /><meshStandardMaterial color="#3b3426" /></mesh>
        </RigidBody>
        <mesh position={[-1.65,.53,.8]}><sphereGeometry args={[.28,24,24]} /><meshStandardMaterial ref={warning} color="#49676b" emissive={accent} emissiveIntensity={.65} metalness={.65} roughness={.25} /></mesh>
        <mesh position={[0,2.7,-.34]}><ringGeometry args={[.72,.735,48]} /><meshBasicMaterial color={accent} transparent opacity={.3} side={THREE.DoubleSide} /></mesh>
        <mesh position={[0,2.7,-.35]}><ringGeometry args={[.9,.908,48]} /><meshBasicMaterial color={accent} transparent opacity={.17} side={THREE.DoubleSide} /></mesh>
      </group>
    }
    
    const defaultPins: Terminal[] = [
      {id:'%IW0',label:'Target angle',slot:1,state:'UNKNOWN'}, {id:'%IW1',label:'Joint angle',slot:1,state:'UNKNOWN'},
      {id:'%IW2',label:'Angular velocity',slot:1,state:'UNKNOWN'}, {id:'%IW3',label:'Core temperature',slot:1,state:'UNKNOWN'},
      {id:'%QW0',label:'Motor PWM',slot:2,state:'UNKNOWN'}, {id:'%QX0.0',label:'Emergency relay',slot:3,state:'UNKNOWN'}, {id:'%QX0.1',label:'Cooling relay',slot:3,state:'UNKNOWN'},
    ]
    export default function WorkbenchScene({ terminals, frame, selected, onSelect }: { terminals?: Terminal[]; frame: MutableRefObject<Frame>; selected: string | null; onSelect: (terminal: Terminal) => void }) {
      return <Canvas shadows dpr={[1,1.5]} camera={{ position:[0,5.2,13.5], fov:40, near:.1, far:100 }} onCreated={({camera}) => camera.lookAt(0,3.5,0)}>
        <color attach="background" args={['#0a1217']} />
        <fog attach="fog" args={['#0a1217', 20, 43]} />
        <ambientLight intensity={1.1} /><directionalLight castShadow position={[3,10,8]} intensity={2.2} shadow-mapSize={[1024,1024]} />
        <pointLight position={[-6,5,3]} color="#68c9ff" intensity={25} distance={15}/><pointLight position={[6,5,3]} color="#6effc5" intensity={22} distance={15}/>
        <Suspense fallback={null}>
          <Grid position={[0,-.09,0]} args={[36,36]} cellSize={.5} cellThickness={.5} cellColor="#365655" sectionSize={2} sectionThickness={.8} sectionColor="#42615d" fadeDistance={22} fadeStrength={1} infiniteGrid />
          <Cabinet terminals={terminals?.length ? terminals : defaultPins} frame={frame} selected={selected} onSelect={onSelect}/>

    # Cloud environment networking
    
    ## Network proxy
    
    > **Network requests go through an isolated proxy sidecar.**
    
    Use the supplied `HTTP_PROXY`, `HTTPS_PROXY`, and CA trust settings with TLS
    verification enabled. The proxy routes outbound HTTP/HTTPS traffic, checks
    destinations, and applies configured credentials to matching HTTPS requests.
    HTTPS usually uses TLS inspection with the supplied CA; configured passthrough
    routes preserve end-to-end TLS. Check route compatibility for clients with
    certificate pinning or custom trust stores.
    
    The default agent command runs inside bubblewrap. Its inherited proxy variables
    can point to a loopback bridge that forwards to the managed proxy. In either
    HTTP policy mode, keep the values supplied to the command, including lowercase
    variants and `NO_PROXY`. To test access, run `curl --max-time 20 "$service_url"`
    with the actual destination URL. If a client requires an explicit proxy setting,
    use the inherited value for that protocol.
    
    The sidecar addresses in the policy snapshot are for the outer executor. Only
    an approved agent command running outside bubblewrap can dial them directly.
    If a sandboxed probe resolves `proxy` but reports “Network is unreachable” for
    `proxy:8080`, retry the destination with inherited settings before diagnosing
    an outage or requesting a policy change. Reserve direct probes for questions
    about the sidecar itself. Escalation still leaves Cloud destination policy in force.
    
    The proxy resolves destination hostnames. In restricted environments, local DNS
    lookups can fail while proxied HTTP succeeds; diagnose connectivity with the
    actual proxied request. Supplied `NO_PROXY` settings keep loopback traffic local.
    
    Outbound rules govern services the environment connects to. Incoming access to
    services running in the environment uses the platform's separate preview or
    port-forwarding controls.
    
    ## Runtime policy
    
    Use these sources to inspect runtime policy:
    
    - `cloud_environment.environment_status`: environment configuration, capabilities, and observed readiness. Refresh after setup or configuration changes.
    - `/etc/codex/network-policy.json`: startup policy and proxy settings; this snapshot can be stale.
    - `http://proxy:8099/status`: live network policy, VPN settings, and routes. If live status is needed, run this direct probe in the outer executor using an approved escalated command: `curl --noproxy proxy -fsS --max-time 5 http://proxy:8099/status`.
    
    For restricted HTTP access, `network_policy.allowed_hosts` contains custom host
    rules, and `presets` lists enabled host bundles. When present,
    `preset_allowed_hosts` contains resolved preset rules and `effective_allowed_hosts`
    contains the complete desired HTTP allowlist. These fields describe desired
    configuration; require `observations_current: true` and
    `network_policy.state: enforced` before treating it as applied. The local file's
    `http_network_policy.egress_rules` provides the startup expansion.
    
    Exact HTTP host rules cover that hostname across paths, methods, and ports.
    `*.example.com` covers subdomains; include `example.com` for the apex. Include
    redirect destinations such as download, CDN, and authentication hosts.
    
    Both HTTP modes apply platform destination blocks and protect proxy-local
    addresses. Environment registration and initial repository checkout use separate,
    scoped platform access rules.
    
    HTTP policy updates apply to subsequent authorization checks while existing
    tunnels and streams continue. After a user saves changes during onboarding,
    call `cloud_environment.environment_status` again and validate access with a fresh connection.
    
    ## Credentials and outbound identities
    
    Use status-reported secret bindings, runtime variable names, and identity aliases
    to plan commands. Keep credential values private and use metadata for diagnosis.
    Use configured SDKs and CLIs normally: the proxy translates credential placeholders
    on matching HTTPS routes. Destination access follows the network policy;
    credentials follow each binding's destination and request scope, including
    scheme, hostname, port, method, and path.
    
    For CLI or SDK profile selection, read the identity metadata manifest at
    `OIC_MANIFEST_PATH` when present. Its version 1 JSON contains a `connections` list.
    Select the entry whose `alias` matches the task's intended identity; `is_default`
    marks the provider's default. Set these variables for that command from the
    selected entry:
    
    | `provider_kind` | Environment variable ← manifest field |
    | --- | --- |
    | `azure` | `IDENTITY_ENDPOINT` ← `identity_endpoint`; `AZURE_CONFIG_DIR` ← `config_dir` |
    | `aws` | `AWS_PROFILE` ← `alias`; `AWS_CONFIG_FILE` ← `config_file` |
    | `gcp` | `CLOUDSDK_CONFIG` ← `config_dir`; `CLOUDSDK_ACTIVE_CONFIG_NAME` ← `configuration_name`; `GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS` ← `credentials_file` |
    
    Preserve inherited authentication headers, guard variables, and injected
    credentials. Use credential file paths as selectors. Check the entry's `setup`
    outcome when diagnosing CLI failures, and report missing or unsupported manifest
    versions, identities, or selector fields.
    
    ## Request failures
    
    Inspect the response body and headers to distinguish proxy limits from remote
    service errors. The proxy applies rate, size, and duration limits. Honor
    `Retry-After` on proxy `429` responses. For `503` responses saying
    `Request size too large` or `Average request size too large`, reduce request size
    or upload rate respectively. Effective limits depend on the configured route.
    
    ## Configured VPN access
    
    Read [Tailscale VPN access](vpn.md) when the local policy reports
    `vpn_configured: true` or when explicitly diagnosing VPN or private-network
    access, even if the flag is missing or false. It covers the supported HTTP and
    TCP routes, connection checks, and troubleshooting. When the local snapshot has
    `automatic_tailscale_access: true`, Tailscale destinations need no HTTP or TCP
    grant; HTTP policy governs non-Tailscale connections. Older environments use
    the separate `tcp_network_access` domain and IP-range grants. Raw TCP always
    uses application-supplied authentication.
